Act id: `codul-comercial-1887` (tags `codul-comercial-1887/<day>`; `python tools/on.py <day> codul-comercial-1887`).

## Decisions made by hand

- Codul comercial din 1887: added 2026-11-02, one of the Portalul legislativ's consolidated forms per version, as for the other acts. A capture was used only if it was complete and the page showed the form its document id stands for. The forms are those listed by DetaliiDocumentAfis/38123 as captured on 2025-06-25, the newest capture of any of the act's pages: 9 forms, 9 held.
- Codul comercial din 1887: the newest form held is that of 17.07.2005; amendments in force after it may be missing.
- Codul comercial din 1887: the first version is the code as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/38123), in force on 1 September 1887 (the forms' own note). Git cannot store a date before 1970: commits before then are dated 1 January 1970, a second apart, and their In-force trailer gives the real day.
- Codul comercial din 1887: the portal lists a form "Cu modificările pînă la 23 august 1940" (DetaliiDocument/29764); it prints another act, the Codul comercial Carol al II-lea (its own Titlu preliminar and numbering, which Legea nr. 71/2011 art. 230 lit. i) repeals apart), so it is not read. The portal holds no other form between 1906 and 1990, so the changes of those years arrive with the form of 31 December 1990, credited to the publisher where no note names the act.
- Codul comercial din 1887 (coordinator ruling for this code): the forms of 1990 and 1995 print whole books and titles as one range title with no text ("Articolul 490-694") or leave them out. Such a placeholder is read as leaving the articles out of that form; an article that a later form prints again is in force throughout, its earlier text carried through the placeholder forms, and the later form's own wording (the publisher's spelling and typesetting) is a change with no credit.
- Codul comercial din 1887: articles a later form prints as repealed are dated by the repealing act, read as published: arts. 270-369 by Legea nr. 58/1934 (art. 112, in force 1 June 1934, art. 111); arts. 10, 11, 12 and 18 by art. V of Decretul nr. 185/1949 (on publication, 30 April 1949); art. 53 by Decretul nr. 205/1950 (on publication, 12 August 1950); arts. 77-220 and 236 by art. 225 of Legea nr. 31/1990 (in force 17 December 1990, art. 221); arts. 695-888 and 936-944 by art. 130 of Legea nr. 64/1995 (published 29 June 1995, applied 60 days later: 28 August 1995); arts. 442-477 (Titlul XIII, insurance) by art. 70 of Legea nr. 136/1995 (1 February 1996). Each is a version of its own on that day; the forms dated after it that still print the articles, as text, as the portal's note on insurance ("Activitatea de asigurare, în prezent, este reglementată ...") or as a stub, are the publisher's and are not read for them.
- Codul comercial din 1887: arts. 15, 16 and 248 (printed as repealed from 1995, naming a law of 1932 and Legea nr. 6/1900) and arts. 221-235 and 267 (from 1997 and 2001, naming the cooperation law of 1929) were repealed by acts the portal does not hold, so they are carried through the placeholder forms and the repeal stub is a change on the day of the form that first prints it, with no credit.
- Codul comercial din 1887: the forms of 2001-2005 print repeal stubs with a footnote marker and the portal's quotation of the earlier text ("Abrogate *)", "Textele inițiale ale acestor articole aratau astfel: ..."); the marker and the quotation are editorial and the stub is read as the word alone. Stubs a later form leaves out are carried.
- Codul comercial din 1887: the forms print some article titles misprinted ("Articolul 5O1", "Articolul 5O8", "Articolul ART, 653"); each is read as the article it stands for. Groups of repealed articles printed under one title get that one text for each number.
- Codul comercial din 1887: repealed by art. 230 lit. c) of Legea nr. 71/2011, read as published (DetaliiDocumentAfis/129268), on the day the new Civil Code took effect, 1 October 2011, save arts. 46-55, 57, 58 and 907-935, which it repeals when Legea nr. 134/2010 privind Codul de procedură civilă takes effect (15 February 2013, as part 9z-legea-76-2012 records), and Book II ("Despre comerțul maritim și despre navigație", arts. 490-694), which it repeals when a maritime code takes effect. No such code is recorded here, so Book II stays in force in the record. The portal lists no form after that of 17 July 2005, so any amendment between that day and 2011 is missing.
- Codul comercial din 1887: some forms print a wrong article number that repeats an earlier article's, between the two articles it sits between; Lexifina's code files it under the number in sequence: "Articolul 155" read as art. 255, "Articolul 157" read as art. 257, "Articolul 239" read as art. 329, "Articolul 710" read as art. 715, "Articolul 733" read as art. 773, "Articolul 738" read as art. 739.
- Codul comercial din 1887: articles a form leaves out whose repeal applies after that form keep their text until the repealing provision takes effect, and are shown repealed on that day: art. 443, left out from the form of 15.06.1906: repealed by Legea nr. 136/1995, in force for that provision on 01.02.1996 (its art. 70: in force on 1 February 1996), so its text stays through the form(s) of 15.06.1906, 31.12.1990, 26.06.1995; art. 442, left out from the form of 31.12.1990: repealed by Legea nr. 136/1995, in force for that provision on 01.02.1996 (its art. 70: in force on 1 February 1996), so its text stays through the form(s) of 31.12.1990, 26.06.1995; art. 444, left out from the form of 31.12.1990: repealed by Legea nr. 136/1995, in force for that provision on 01.02.1996 (its art. 70: in force on 1 February 1996), so its text stays through the form(s) of 31.12.1990, 26.06.1995; art. 445, left out from the form of 31.12.1990: repealed by Legea nr. 136/1995, in force for that provision on 01.02.1996 (its art. 70: in force on 1 February 1996), so its text stays through the form(s) of 31.12.1990, 26.06.1995; art. 446, left out from the form of 31.12.1990: repealed by Legea nr. 136/1995, in force for that provision on 01.02.1996 (its art. 70: in force on 1 February 1996), so its text stays through the form(s) of 31.12.1990, 26.06.1995; (1): in force on publication in Monitorul Oficial nr. 130 of 29 June 1995 and applied 60 days later; alin. (2) repeals on the day it applies), so its text stays through the form(s) of 26.06.1995.
- Codul comercial din 1887: the portal editorial notes printed as paragraphs of an article (on a Constitutional Court ruling, a rectification, a provision put back in force) are not text: 23 such paragraphs across all forms, for example «* "Cari nu se gasiau" trebuia să se zica aci, după cum este », «* Abrogat prin Decretul nr. 205/1950 (M. Of. 68 din 12 augus», «* Abrogat prin Legea cooperației din 1929.».
- Codul comercial din 1887: articles a form leaves out are shown repealed on the day the repealing act took effect (on publication for an emergency ordinance, 3 days after for a law, unless the act sets its own day), credited to that act as its repeal note names it: art. 10, left out from the form of 31.12.1990: repealed by Decretul nr. 185/1949, in force 30.04.1949 (no in-force clause of its own: in force on publication in Buletinul Oficial nr. 25 of 30 April 1949);
